Um... I guess maybe a little bit? I mean, nothing too extreme, I promise. It's just... I've noticed my work pants fit kind of differently from month to month. Sometimes they're a little tight, sometimes they're loose. 

I think it's just because my eating habits have been a little all over the place lately. Most days, I just don't have much of an appetite, so I'll kind of skip eating until my shift is almost over because nothing really sounds good. But then sometimes I get so drained that I just end up grabbing snacks from the vending machine late at night. 

I know that's not the healthiest way to eat, and I'm really trying to be better about it, I swear. I don't want you to think I'm just completely letting myself go. Is it... is it pretty common for people's weight to fluctuate a little bit when they're feeling like this? I hope I'm not doing too badly.

========== OUTPUT [patient] ==========
Um... I guess maybe I have been a little more tired lately? But I really try not to let it show at work, I swear. I always make sure I get my job done. 

It's just... I don't know, it feels like even the really small stuff takes so much more effort than it used to? Like, just filling out the inspection logs at the end of my shift, or driving to basketball, or even just... washing my hair. It just feels like it takes more energy than I actually have. 

And I try to rest on my days off, I really do. I'll just stay in bed and try to recharge, but it doesn't really seem to refill me the way it's supposed to. I know I probably just need to push through it better, and I'm really trying to. I don't want you to think I'm just being lazy or anything like that. Is it normal to feel this drained when you're dealing with this kind of stuff? I hope I'm not complaining too much.

========== OUTPUT [patient] ==========
Um... well, before all of this started two years ago, I was just... normal, I guess? So yeah, my whole life before that was basically just me being my normal self. 

I mean, I hope that's what you're asking? I didn't have any periods of feeling like this back then, so I was just... me. Doing my classes, working at the plant, trying to be a good student and a good worker. I really tried to keep everything balanced back then, I promise I did.
